  SA 5179. Mr. KAINE submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4109. CONGRESSIONAL BUDGET OFFICE ANALYSIS AND REPORT ON 
                   THE ECONOMIC AND FISCAL COSTS OF THE TRUMP 
                   ADMINISTRATION'S IMMIGRATION ENFORCEMENT 
                   MEASURES.

       (a) Report.--Not later than 180 days after the date on 
     which this resolution is adopted by Congress, the 
     Congressional Budget Office shall submit to a report to 
     Congress that analyzes the economic and fiscal costs of the 
     Trump Administration's increased immigration enforcement 
     measures, including--
       (1) the impact of such measures on the Federal budget;
       (2) costs incurred by States and local governments in 
     response to such measures, such as--
       (A) law enforcement overtime pay;
       (B) costs associated with purchasing and converting 
     warehouses into detention and processing facilities; and
       (C) increased security and other costs at schools, 
     courthouses, and other State and local facilities;
       (3) the impact of such measures on school attendance, 
     student mental health, test scores, and college enrollment;
       (4) the economic impact of such measures on--
       (A) the housing sector, including constructions costs;
       (B) the agricultural sector, including food costs; and
       (C) the home health care sector, including the availability 
     of workers, particularly in light of an aging population.
       (b) Public Availability.--The Congressional Budget Office 
     shall make the report required under subsection (a) publicly 
     available on its website, but may exclude any classified or 
     sensitive information.
                                 ______
                                 
  SA 5180. Mr. KAINE submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST COLLECTING THE HEALTH AND 
                   MEDICAL RECORDS OF WORKERS.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, amendment, 
     amendment between the Houses, or conference report that 
     funds--
       (1) the collection or publication of the health and medical 
     records of any individual enrolled in a health benefits plan 
     under chapter 89 of title 5, United States Code, including a 
     plan under the Federal Employees Health Benefits Program or 
     the Postal Service Health Benefits Program, by the Office of 
     Personnel Management or any other Federal agency; or
       (2) the communication of health and medical records 
     described in paragraph (1) to the

[[Page S2002]]

     Office of Personnel Management or any other Federal agency.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

  SA 5184. Mr. KAINE submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

         At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. DON'T MAKE AMERICA SICK AGAIN.

         (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the 
     Senate to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, 
     amendment, amendment between the Houses, or conference report 
     that would make America sick again, as described in 
     subsection (b).
         (b) Legislation Described.--For purposes of subsection 
     (a), a bill, joint resolution, motion, amendment, amendment 
     between the Houses, or conference report that would make 
     America sick again means a bill, joint resolution, motion, 
     amendment, amendment between the Houses, or conference report 
     that the Congressional Budget Office determines would--
         (1) reduce the number of Americans enrolled in public or 
     private health insurance coverage, as determined based on the 
     latest updated baseline budget projections by the 
     Congressional Budget Office;
         (2) increase health insurance premiums or total out-of-
     pocket health care costs for Americans with private health 
     insurance; or
         (3) reduce the scope and scale of benefits covered by 
     private health insurance, as compared to the benefits 
     Americans would have received pursuant to the requirements 
     under title I of the Patient Protection and Affordable Care 
     Act (Public Law 111-148; 124 Stat. 130) and the amendments 
     made by that title.
         (c) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).
                                 ______

     Development to act in a manner inconsistent with the 
     legislative intent of section 214 of the Housing and 
     Community Development Act of 1980 (42 U.S.C. 1436a).

     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under

congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO LOWERING 
                   GROCERY PRICES.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     lowering grocery prices by prohibiting the grocery retail 
     industry from engaging in unfair, deceptive, or 
     anticompetitive practices that increase grocery prices or 
     reduce access to food, which may include restrictive 
     covenants or surveillance pricing, by the amounts provided in 
     such legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.

  SA 5218. Mr. HEINRICH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST LEGISLATION THAT WOULD 
                   SUBSIDIZE UNECONOMIC COAL-FIRED POWER PLANTS.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, amendment, 
     amendment between the Houses, or conference report that would 
     subsidize uneconomic coal-fired power plants.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

  SA 5226. Mr. HEINRICH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST LEGISLATION THAT WOULD 
                   INCREASE THE COST OF ELECTRICITY BILLS FOR 
                   AMERICAN CITIZENS.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, amendment, 
     amendment between the Houses, or conference report that would 
     increase the cost of electricity bills for American citizens.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).
                                 ______

  SA 5228. Ms. BALDWIN submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by her to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST ALLOWING AGENTS OR OFFICERS 
                   OF U.S. IMMIGRATION AND CUSTOMS ENFORCEMENT OR 
                   U.S. CUSTOMS AND BORDER PROTECTION WHO ARE ON 
                   DUTY OR IN UNIFORM, DURING ANY VOTING PERIOD, 
                   TO ENTER ANY LOCATION WITHIN \1/4\ MILE OF AN 
                   ACTIVE POLLING PLACE, TO SET UP A CHECKPOINT 
                   ALONG ANY ROUTE TO AN ACTIVE POLLING PLACE, OR 
                   TO INTERFERE IN ANY WAY WITH THE ABILITY OF ANY 
                   ELIGIBLE VOTER TO ACCESS HIS OR HER ASSIGNED 
                   POLLING PLACE.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, amendment, 
     amendment between the Houses, or conference report that would 
     allow for agents or officers of U.S. Immigration and Customs 
     Enforcement or U.S. Customs and Border Protection who are on 
     duty or in uniform, during any voting period--
       (1) to enter any location within \1/4\ mile of an active 
     polling place;
       (2) to set up a checkpoint along any route to an active 
     polling place; or
       (3) to interfere in any way with the ability of any 
     eligible voter to access his or her assigned polling place.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).
                                 ______
                                 
  SA 5229. Ms. BALDWIN submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by her to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO ELECTION 
                   SECURITY.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     election security, which may include a prohibition on making 
     funds available for agents or officers of U.S. Immigration 
     and Customs Enforcement or U.S. Customs and Border Protection 
     who are on duty or in uniform, during any voting period, from 
     entering any location within \1/4\ mile of an active polling 
     place, setting up a checkpoint along any route to an active 
     polling place, or interfering in any way with the ability of 
     any eligible voter to access his or her assigned polling 
     place, by the amounts provided in such legislation for those 
     purposes, provided that such legislation would not increase 
     the deficit over the period of the total of fiscal years 2026 
     through 2035.

her to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   REDIRECTING AND RESCINDING CERTAIN FUNDS 
                   APPROPRIATED UNDER THE ONE BIG BEAUTIFUL BILL 
                   ACT SO THAT NOT ONE MORE CHILD LOSES ACCESS TO 
                   HEALTH CARE.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to health 
     care for children, which may include redirecting and 
     rescinding funds appropriated under sections 90003 and 100052 
     of Public Law 119-21 (commonly known as the ``One Big 
     Beautiful Bill Act''), including rescinding funds 
     appropriated for the Department of Homeland Security, U.S. 
     Immigration and Customs Enforcement, or U.S. Customs and 
     Border Protection, repealing health care cuts enacted under 
     the One Big Beautiful Bill Act, guaranteeing funding for the 
     Rural Health Transformation Program established in the 
     amendments made by section 71401 of such Act, including by 
     making funds allotted to States under such program remain 
     available until expended and eliminating any redistribution 
     of unexpended or unobligated funds from such allotments, and 
     sustaining sections 71202, 71306, and 71401 of such Act and 
     the amendments made by such sections, by the amounts provided 
     in such legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   PROTECTING VETERANS FROM GUARDIANSHIP 
                   AGREEMENTS THAT THREATEN THEIR RIGHTS, DIGNITY, 
                   AND AUTONOMY.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     protecting veterans from being wrongfully or unnecessarily 
     placed in legal guardianships or conservatorships, which may 
     include legislation to ensure the memorandum of understanding 
     between the Department of Justice and the Department of 
     Veterans Affairs regarding guardianship or conservatorship 
     proceedings includes a requirement for independent medical or 
     psychological evaluations when determining a veteran's 
     incapacity, to ensure other ethical safeguards are in place, 
     or to ensure due process protections are afforded to 
     veterans, including notice, right to counsel, and 
     opportunities to contest guardianship or conservatorship 
     determinations, by the amounts provided in such legislation 
     for those purposes, provided that such legislation would not

  SA 5249. Mr. WELCH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST RECONCILIATION LEGISLATION 
                   THAT WOULD ALLOW AN INDIVIDUAL CONVICTED OF AN 
                   OFFENSE RELATING TO THE ATTACK ON THE UNITED 
                   STATES CAPITOL ON JANUARY 6, 2021, TO BE 
                   APPOINTED TO A POSITION OF FEDERAL EMPLOYMENT 
                   OR ACCESS FEDERAL PROPERTY.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider a bill or joint resolution reported pursuant to 
     section 2002, or an amendment to, conference report on, or 
     amendment between the Houses in relation to such a bill or 
     joint resolution, that, with respect to any individual 
     convicted of an offense relating to the attack on the United 
     States Capitol on January 6, 2021--
       (1) would authorize or appropriate funding to appoint such 
     an individual to a position in the Federal Government, 
     including a position in any component, agency, or other 
     office of the Federal Government;
       (2) does not deem such an individual ineligible for 
     employment or other financial engagement with the Federal 
     Government, including with any component, agency, or other 
     office of the Federal Government; or
       (3) does not deny such an individual access to property of 
     the Federal Government, including property of any component, 
     agency, or other office of the Federal Government.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

     SEC. 4109. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST LEGISLATION PROVIDING 
                   FEDERAL FUNDING FOR U.S. IMMIGRATION AND 
                   CUSTOMS ENFORCEMENT OR U.S. CUSTOMS AND BORDER 
                   PROTECTION IF AN EMPLOYEE OF EITHER AGENCY 
                   KILLED, ARRESTED, DETAINED, OR HELD IN CUSTODY 
                   A UNITED STATES CITIZEN.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider a bill or joint resolution reported pursuant to 
     section 2002, or an amendment to, conference report on, or 
     amendment between the Houses in relation to such a bill or 
     joint resolution, that would provide funding through budget 
     reconciliation for U.S. Immigration and Customs Enforcement 
     or U.S. Customs and Border Protection if an employee of 
     either agency is determined to have killed, arrested, 
     detained, or held in custody a United States citizen during 
     the most recently concluded fiscal year.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year

follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST RECONCILIATION LEGISLATION 
                   THAT WOULD ADVERSELY IMPACT THE RIGHT OF WOMEN 
                   TO VOTE IF ENACTED.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider a bill or joint resolution reported pursuant to 
     section 2002, or an amendment to, conference report on, or 
     amendment between the Houses in relation to such a bill or 
     joint resolution, if the reconciliation legislation contains 
     voting provisions, including provisions related to voter 
     registration or ballot access, that could reasonably be 
     considered to adversely impact the right of women to vote, 
     including legislation that implements barriers to voting for 
     women who have changed their names due to marriage or other 
     obstacles that can be demonstrated to impact women who are 
     eligible to vote.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

                        TITLE II--RESERVE FUNDS

     SEC. 2001. RESERVE FUND FOR REFORMS TO U.S. IMMIGRATION AND 
                   CUSTOMS ENFORCEMENT AND U.S. BORDER PATROL.

         (a) Senate.--The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget 
     of the Senate may revise the allocations of a committee or 
     committees, aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this 
     resolution, including, consistent with section 4101(b)(1), 
     the committee allocations to the Committee on Appropriations 
     of the Senate for fiscal year 2026, and make adjustments to 
     the pay-as-you-go ledger for one or more bills or joint 
     resolutions reported by the Committee on the Judiciary of the 
     Senate or the Committee on Homeland Security and Governmental 
     Affairs of the Senate, amendments, amendments between the 
     Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to funding 
     and accompanying reforms to immigration enforcement 
     activities, which may include reforms to the training, 
     conduct, operations, investigations, and oversight of U.S. 
     Immigration and Customs Enforcement and U.S. Border Patrol, 
     by the amounts of budget authority and resulting outlays 
     provided in such legislation for those purposes in fiscal 
     year 2026.
         (b) House of Representatives.--The Chairman of the 
     Committee on the Budget of the House of Representatives may 
     revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     including, consistent with section 4101(a)(1), the committee 
     allocations to the Committee on Appropriations of the House 
     of Representatives for fiscal year 2026, and make adjustments 
     to the pay-as-you-go ledger for one or more bills, joint 
     resolutions, amendments, amendments between the Houses, 
     motions, or conference reports relating to funding and 
     accompanying reforms related to immigration enforcement 
     activities, which may include reforms to the training, 
     conduct, operations, investigations, and oversight of the 
     U.S. Immigration and Customs Enforcement and U.S. Border 
     Patrol, by the amounts of budget authority and resulting 
     outlays provided in such legislation for those purposes in 
     fiscal year 2026.

by her to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   PRIORITIZING NEW FUNDS FOR U.S. CUSTOMS AND 
                   BORDER PROTECTION FOR TARIFF REFUND PROCESSING.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     prioritizing new funds for U.S. Customs and Border Protection 
     for processing refunds of tariffs, by the amounts provided in 
     such legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.

     SEC. 3003. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO THE 
                   APPREHENSION AND DEPORTATION OF ADULT ILLEGAL 
                   ALIENS CONVICTED OF SEXUAL ABUSE OF A MINOR 
                   AFTER ILLEGALLY ENTERING THE UNITED STATES.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills or joint resolutions reported by the Committee on 
     the Judiciary or the Committee on Homeland Security and 
     Governmental Affairs of the Senate, amendments or motions 
     offered thereto, or conference reports submitted thereon 
     relating to immigration enforcement, which may include 
     legislation funding U.S. Immigration and Customs Enforcement 
     personnel to conduct apprehension, mandatory detention, and 
     expedited deportation of adult illegal aliens who have been 
     convicted of sexual abuse of a minor after illegally entering 
     the United States, by the amounts provided in such 
     legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.

  SA 5296. Ms. HASSAN (for herself and Mr. Warnock) submitted an 
amendment intended to be proposed by her to the concurrent resolution 
S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the congressional budget for the United 
States Government for fiscal year 2026 and setting forth the 
appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal years 2027 through 2035; which 
was ordered to lie on the table; as follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST RECONCILIATION LEGISLATION 
                   THAT WOULD NOT LOWER OUT-OF-POCKET PRESCRIPTION 
                   DRUG COSTS.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider a bill or joint resolution reported pursuant to 
     section 2002, or an amendment to, conference report on, or 
     amendment between the Houses in relation to such a bill or 
     joint resolution, that would not lower out-of-pocket 
     prescription drug costs.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).
                                 ______

  SA 5298. Ms. CANTWELL submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by her to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, insert the following:

     SEC. 41__. CONGRESSIONAL BUDGET OFFICE STUDY ON TRANSPARENCY 
                   OF SIZE OF ICE AND CBP FUNDING IN THE 119TH 
                   CONGRESS.

       Not later than 30 days after the date on which this 
     resolution is agreed to, the Congressional Budget Office 
     shall submit to Congress, and make publicly available, a 
     report that makes the amounts made available for U.S. 
     Immigration and Customs Enforcement and U.S. Customs and 
     Border Protection during the 119th Congress easier to 
     understand and communicate by equating such amounts to the 
     number of days programs funded by the Highway Trust Fund 
     could be funded if an equal amount of funding were deposited 
     in the Highway Trust Fund.
                                 ______
                                 
  SA 5299. Ms. CANTWELL submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by her to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4____. CONGRESSIONAL BUDGET OFFICE CALCULATION AND 
                   ANALYSIS ON THE COST OF INCREASED GAS PRICES ON 
                   FAMILIES IN THE UNITED STATES.

       Not later than 30 days after the date on which this 
     resolution is adopted by Congress, the Congressional Budget 
     Office shall submit to Congress, and make publicly available, 
     a report that calculates and analyzes the cost to families in 
     the United States resulting from increased gas prices since 
     February 28, 2026.

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   PROTECTING CERTAIN RELATIVES OF CURRENT AND

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees,

     posting of denial information without personally identifiable 
     information, by the amounts provided in such legislation for

     increase the deficit over the period of the total of fiscal 
     years 2026 through 2035.

                   DEPARTMENT OF VETERANS AFFAIRS.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees,

     individual served a term of imprisonment of at least 5 years,

     purposes, provided that such legislation would not increase 
     the deficit over the period of the total of fiscal years 2026 
     through 2035.

  SA 5314. Mr. BENNET submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO ENSURING 
                   FAIR, TRANSPARENT, AND TIMELY ACCESS TO MAJOR 
                   DISASTER ASSISTANCE.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     ensuring fair, transparent, and timely access to major 
     disaster assistance under the Robert T. Stafford Disaster 
     Relief and Emergency Assistance Act (42 U.S.C. 5121 et seq.), 
     which may include through establishing objective evaluation 
     criteria, improved appeals processes, and temporary recovery 
     or hazard mitigation assistance following the denial of a 
     request or appeal, by the amounts provided in such 
     legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
                                 ______

him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

         At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   REDIRECTING U.S. IMMIGRATION AND CUSTOMS 
                   ENFORCEMENT FUNDING TOWARDS DEPARTMENT OF THE 
                   INTERIOR AND FOREST SERVICE HAZARDOUS FUELS 
                   REDUCTION ACTIVITIES.

         The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     immigration enforcement, which shall include redirecting U.S. 
     Immigration and Customs Enforcement funding towards 
     Department of the Interior and Forest Service hazardous fuels 
     reduction projects over a 10-year period, if those projects 
     are carried out in accordance with existing Federal law, by 
     the amounts provided in such legislation for those purposes, 
     provided that such legislation would not increase the deficit 
     over the period of the total of fiscal years 2026 through 
     2035.

  SA 5331. Mr. HEINRICH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035;

[[Page S2023]]

which was ordered to lie on the table; as follows:

         At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST LEGISLATION THAT WOULD 
                   UNDERMINE PREPAREDNESS FOR THE 2026 FIRE 
                   SEASON.

         (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the 
     Senate to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, 
     amendment, amendment between the Houses, or conference report 
     that would undermine preparedness for the 2026 fire season.
         (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

         At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO MAKING 
                   CHILDCARE MORE AFFORDABLE.

         The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to making 
     child care more affordable, which may include increasing 
     Federal funding for childcare providers, increasing funding 
     to States and local governments administering childcare 
     programs, providing tax relief to families paying for 
     caregiving services, and further studying ways to improve 
     workforce participation and compensation, by the amounts 
     provided in such legislation for those purposes, provided 
     that such legislation would not increase the deficit over the 
     period of the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.

  SA 5346. Mr. BENNET (for himself and Mr. Hickenlooper) submitted an 
amendment intended to be proposed by him to the concurrent resolution 
S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the congressional budget for the United 
States Government for fiscal year 2026 and setting forth the 
appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal years 2027 through 2035; which 
was ordered to lie on the table; as follows:

         At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   REINSTATING CERTAIN DISCRETIONARY COMPETITIVE 
                   GRANTS FOR THE STATE OF COLORADO THAT HAVE BEEN 
                   WITHDRAWN, CANCELLED, OR TERMINATED ON OR AFTER 
                   JANUARY 20, 2025.

         (a) In General.--The Chairman of the Committee on the 
     Budget of the Senate may revise the allocations of a 
     committee or committees, aggregates, and other appropriate 
     levels in this resolution, and make adjustments to the pay-
     as-you-go ledger, for one or more bills, joint resolutions, 
     amendments, amendments between the Houses, motions, or 
     conference reports relating to requiring the Secretary of 
     Transportation to reinstate the transportation grants 
     described in subsection (b) by the amounts provided in such 
     legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
         (b) Transportation Grants Described.--The transportation 
     grants referred to in subsection (a) are all discretionary 
     competitive grants for the State of Colorado that have been 
     withdrawn, cancelled, or terminated by the Department of 
     Transportation on or after January 20, 2025, including the 
     following:
         (1) $66,400,000 for the Colorado Department of 
     Transportation for the Modernizing Rail on the Front Range: 
     PTC Installation, Siding, and Grade Crossing Safety and 
     Operational Improvements project.
         (2) $11,700,284 for the City of Fort Collins for the 
     SMART Stage 2-SPARC-FLEET 2030 Smart Power and Resilient 
     Charging for Fleet Electrification and Emissions Targets 
     project.
         (3) $11,671,781 for Colorado State University Pueblo for 
     the Safety Assessment, Testing and Workforce Development for 
     Hydrogen/Natural Gas Motive Power project.
         (4) $8,340,000 for the Colorado Department of 
     Transportation.
         (5) $10,700,000 for the City of Fort Collins to build a 
     transit station and roundabout at the intersection of West 
     Elizabeth Street and South Overland Trail as part of a bus-
     rapid-transit initiative.

  SA 5347. Mr. BOOKER submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

         At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST LEGISLATION THAT WOULD 
                   UNDERMINE CIVIL RIGHTS PROTECTIONS.

         (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the 
     Senate to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, 
     amendment, amendment between the Houses, or conference report 
     that endangers protections for renters, homeowners, or people 
     experiencing homelessness by reducing funding for or staff 
     charged with enforcement of the Fair Housing Act (42 U.S.C. 
     3601 et seq.), the Violence Against Women Act of 1994 (34 
     U.S.C. 12291 et seq.), the Americans with Disabilities Act of 
     1990 ( 42 U.S.C. 12101 et seq.), or other civil rights laws.
         (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between

                        TITLE III--RESERVE FUNDS

     SEC. 3000. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUNDS WITHIN THE 
                   JURISDICTION OF THE COMMITTEE ON RULES AND 
                   ADMINISTRATION.

         (a) Deficit-neutral Reserve Fund Relating to Establishing 
     Identification Requirements for Registration to Vote in 
     Elections for Federal Office.--The Chairman of the Committee 
     on the Budget of the Senate may revise the allocations of a 
     committee or committees, aggregates, and other appropriate 
     levels in this resolution, and make adjustments to the pay-
     as-you-go ledger, for one or more bills, joint resolutions, 
     amendments, amendments between the Houses, motions, or 
     conference reports relating to establishing identification 
     requirements for registration to vote in elections for 
     Federal office, which may include requiring photo 
     identification or an affidavit, by the amounts provided in 
     such legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
         (b) Deficit-neutral Reserve Fund Relating to Establishing 
     Photo Identification Requirements for Voting in Elections for 
     Federal Office.--The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget 
     of the Senate may revise the allocations of a committee or 
     committees, aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this 
     resolution, and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, 
     for one or more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, 
     amendments between the Houses, motions, or conference reports 
     relating to establishing photo identification requirements 
     for voting in elections for Federal office by the amounts 
     provided in such legislation for those purposes, provided 
     that such legislation would not increase the deficit over the 
     period of the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
         (c) Deficit-neutral Reserve Fund Relating to Election Day 
     and the Counting of Ballots in Federal Elections.--The 
     Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate may 
     revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     Federal elections, which may include restrictions on allowing 
     voting other than on election day and requirements that all 
     votes be counted within 36 hours of election day, on by the 
     amounts provided in such legislation for those purposes, 
     provided that such legislation would not increase the deficit 
     over the period of the total of fiscal years 2026 through 
     2035.

  SA 5366. Ms. KLOBUCHAR (for herself and Ms. Smith) submitted an 
amendment intended to be proposed by her to the concurrent resolution 
S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the congressional budget for the United 
States Government for fiscal year 2026 and setting forth the 
appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal years 2027 through 2035; which 
was ordered to lie on the table; as follows:

         At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST RECONCILIATION LEGISLATION 
                   THAT WOULD NOT REIMBURSE STATES AND LOCALITIES 
                   FOR THE COSTS INCURRED AS A RESULT OF EXPANDED 
                   IMMIGRATION ENFORCEMENT OPERATIONS.

         (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the 
     Senate to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, 
     amendment, amendment between the Houses, or conference report 
     that would not reimburse States and localities for costs 
     incurred as a result of expanded immigration enforcement 
     operations, including costs incurred as a result of 
     ``Operation Metro Surge''.
         (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

  SA 5379. Mr. WELCH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   REDIRECTING AND RESCINDING CERTAIN FUNDS 
                   APPROPRIATED UNDER THE ONE BIG BEAUTIFUL BILL 
                   ACT, SO THAT BLOCKBUSTER ORPHAN DRUGS AREN'T 
                   EXEMPT FROM MEDICARE DRUG PRICE NEGOTIATION.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     redirecting and rescinding certain funds appropriated under 
     Public Law 119-21 (commonly known as the ``One Big Beautiful 
     Bill Act''), so that Blockbuster orphan drugs aren't exempt 
     from Medicare drug price negotiation, which may include 
     repealing section 71203 of such Act to ensure that Medicare 
     can negotiate drug prices for rare disease drugs when 
     Medicare spending exceeds $400,000,000 annually on that drug, 
     regardless of the drug's orphan status, and paying for such 
     repeal by rescinding funds appropriated under sections 90003 
     and 100052 of such Act, including rescinding funds 
     appropriated for the Department of Homeland Security, 
     Immigration and Customs Enforcement, or Customs and Border 
     Patrol, by the amounts provided in such legislation for those 
     purposes, provided that such legislation would not increase 
     the deficit over the period of the total of fiscal years 2026 
     through 2035.
                                 ______
                                 
  SA 5380. Mr. BLUMENTHAL submitted an amendment intended to be 
proposed by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting 
forth the congressional budget for the United States Government for 
fiscal year 2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for 
fiscal years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; 
as follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO THE 
                   PROHIBITION OF ADMINISTRATIVE WARRANTS FOR 
                   ENTRY INTO PRIVATE RESIDENCES.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to the 
     use of an administrative warrant to enter a private residence 
     during any immigration enforcement operation, which may 
     include legislation to prohibit the Department of Homeland 
     Security from using Federal funds to use an administrative 
     warrant to enter a private residence during any immigration 
     enforcement operation, by the amounts provided in such 
     legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
                                 ______
                                 
  SA 5381. Mr. BLUMENTHAL submitted an amendment intended to be 
proposed by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting 
forth the congressional budget for the United States Government for 
fiscal year 2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for 
fiscal years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; 
as follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   DEPARTMENT OF JUSTICE AUDITS OR INVESTIGATIONS.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     Department of Justice audits or investigations, which may 
     include audits by the Office of the Inspector General of the 
     Department of Justice of the Department's compliance with the 
     Epstein Files Transparency Act (Public Law 119-38; 139 Stat. 
     656), by the amounts provided in such legislation for those 
     purposes, provided that such legislation would not increase 
     the deficit over the period of the total of fiscal years 2026 
     through 2035.
                                 ______
                                 
  SA 5382. Mr. BLUMENTHAL submitted an amendment intended to be 
proposed by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting 
forth the congressional budget for the United States Government for 
fiscal year 2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for 
fiscal years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; 
as follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3003. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO REVIEWING 
                   THE RECORDS USED TO JUSTIFY DECISIONS TO DEPORT 
                   LAW-ABIDING UNITED STATES SERVICEMEMBERS, 
                   VETERANS, AND THEIR RESPECTIVE FAMILIES FOR 
                   DEPORTATION.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference

[[Page S2032]]

     reports relating to the targeting of law-abiding United 
     States servicemembers, veterans, and their respective 
     families for detention or deportation, which may include 
     legislation requiring the Department of Justice and the 
     Department of Homeland Security to review the records used to 
     justify the deportation of, or to reverse decisions made to 
     deport, law-abiding United States servicemembers, veterans, 
     and their respective families, by the amounts provided in 
     such legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
                                 ______
                                 
  SA 5383. Ms. SMITH (for herself and Ms. Klobuchar) submitted an 
amendment intended to be proposed by her to the concurrent resolution 
S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the congressional budget for the United 
States Government for fiscal year 2026 and setting forth the 
appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal years 2027 through 2035; which 
was ordered to lie on the table; as follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3003. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO ENSURING 
                   COOPERATION BETWEEN FEDERAL AND STATE 
                   INVESTIGATIVE AUTHORITIES REGARDING THE 
                   KILLINGS OF CIVILIANS BY FEDERAL OFFICERS.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     protecting civil rights, which may include requiring greater 
     cooperation between Federal and State investigative 
     authorities with respect to the killings of civilians by 
     Federal officers, by the amounts provided in such legislation 
     for those purposes, provided that such legislation would not 
     increase the deficit over the period of the total of fiscal 
     years 2026 through 2035.
                                 ______
                                 
  SA 5384. Ms. SMITH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
her to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       Strike section 3002 and insert the following:

     SEC. 3002. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND FOR REFORMS FOLLOWING 
                   OPERATION METRO SURGE.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills or joint resolutions reported by the Committee on 
     the Judiciary or the Committee on Homeland Security and 
     Governmental Affairs of the Senate, amendments or motions 
     offered thereto, or conference reports submitted thereon 
     relating to supporting any changes to immigration enforcement 
     policy undertaken following Operation Metro Surge, which may 
     include reforms to protect the rights of individuals under 
     the Constitution of the United States, and to ensure Federal 
     law enforcement officers are held to the same or higher 
     standards to which State and local law enforcement officers 
     are subject, by the amounts provided in such legislation for 
     those purposes, provided that such legislation would not 
     increase the deficit over the period of the total of fiscal

     2035.

him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. SUPERMAJORITY ENFORCEMENT OF UNFUNDED MANDATES IN 
                   THE SENATE.

       Notwithstanding section 904(b) of the Congressional Budget 
     Act of 1974 (2 U.S.C. 621 note), in the Senate, paragraphs 
     (1) and (2) of section 425(a) of the Congressional Budget Act 
     of 1974 (2 U.S.C. 658d(a)) shall be subject to the waiver and 
     appeal requirements of subsections (c)(2) and (d)(3), 
     respectively, of section 904 of the Congressional Budget Act 
     of 1974 (2 U.S.C. 621 note).

  SA 5398. Mr. REED submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

         Strike sections 2001 and 2002, and insert the following:

     SEC. 2001. RECONCILIATION IN THE HOUSE OF REPRESENTATIVES.

         (a) Submissions.--In the House of Representatives, not 
     later than May 15, 2026, the committees named in subsection 
     (b) shall submit their recommendations on changes in laws 
     within their jurisdictions to the Committee on the Budget of 
     the House of Representatives to carry out this section.
         (b) Instructions.--
         (1) Committee on ways and means.--The Committee on Ways 
     and Means of the House of Representatives shall report 
     changes in laws within its jurisdiction that increase the 
     deficit by not more than $500,000,000,000 for the period of 
     fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
         (2) Committee on energy and commerce.--The Committee on 
     Energy and Commerce of the House of Representatives shall 
     report changes in laws within its jurisdiction that increase 
     the deficit by not more than $1,000,000,000,000 for the 
     period of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.

     SEC. 2002. RECONCILIATION IN THE SENATE.

         In the Senate, not later than May 15, 2026, the Committee 
     on Finance of the Senate shall report to the Senate changes 
     in laws within its jurisdiction that increase the deficit by 
     not more than $1,500,000,000,000 for the period of fiscal 
     years 2026 through 2035.
                                 ______

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO A 
                   DEPARTMENT OF DEFENSE CIVILIAN WORKFORCE 
                   EXPERIENCE GAP ASSESSMENT.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     national security, including by requiring the Secretary of 
     Defense to conduct a comprehensive assessment of the multi-
     year impacts of Department of Defense civilian workforce 
     reductions from 2025, particularly the resulting experience 
     and skills gaps in cases where personnel losses were not 
     offset by entry-level or mid-level hiring, and may include an 
     evaluation of effects on mission execution, technical 
     expertise, and institutional knowledge across critical 
     functional areas, as well as risks to readiness, acquisition,

                        TITLE III--RESERVE FUNDS

     SEC. 3000. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUNDS WITHIN THE 
                   JURISDICTION OF THE COMMITTEE ON RULES AND 
                   ADMINISTRATION.

       (a) Deficit-neutral Reserve Fund Relating to Establishing 
     Identification Requirements for Registration to Vote in 
     Elections for Federal Office.--The Chairman of the Committee 
     on the Budget of the Senate may revise the allocations of a 
     committee or committees, aggregates, and other appropriate 
     levels in this resolution, and make adjustments to the pay-
     as-you-go ledger, for one or more bills, joint resolutions, 
     amendments, amendments between the Houses, motions, or 
     conference reports relating to establishing identification 
     requirements for registration to vote in elections for 
     Federal office, which may include requiring photo 
     identification, by the amounts provided in such legislation 
     for those purposes, provided that such legislation would not 
     increase the deficit over the period of the total of fiscal 
     years 2026 through 2035.
       (b) Deficit-neutral Reserve Fund Relating to Establishing 
     Photo Identification Requirements for Voting in Elections for 
     Federal Office.--The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget 
     of the Senate may revise the allocations of a committee or 
     committees, aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this 
     resolution, and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, 
     for one or more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, 
     amendments between the Houses, motions, or conference reports 
     relating to establishing photo identification requirements 
     for voting in elections for Federal office by the amounts 
     provided in such legislation for those purposes, provided 
     that such legislation would not increase the deficit over the 
     period of the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
       (c) Deficit-neutral Reserve Fund Relating to Election Day 
     and the Counting of Ballots in Federal Elections.--The 
     Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate may 
     revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     Federal elections, which may include restrictions on allowing 
     voting other than on election day and requirements that all 
     votes be counted within 36 hours of election day, on by the 
     amounts provided in such legislation for those purposes, 
     provided that such legislation would not increase the deficit 
     over the period of the total of fiscal years 2026 through 
     2035.

  SA 5423. Mr. WARNOCK submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST THE USE OF FUNDS TO SUPPORT 
                   ROVING PATROLS AND RACIAL PROFILING.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, amendment, 
     amendment between the Houses, or conference report that would 
     permit the use of funds to patrol and racial profiling.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the

which was ordered to lie on the table; as follows:

       At the appropriate place in title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4___. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST THE DETENTION AND 
                   DEPORTATION OF FAMILY MEMBERS OF UNITED STATES 
                   MILITARY SERVICE MEMBERS AND VETERANS.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, amendment, 
     amendment between the Houses, or conference report that would 
     provide for the detention and deportation of immediate and 
     extended family members of United States military service 
     members and veterans of the United States Armed Forces, 
     except in cases of detention for felony violations of United 
     States criminal law.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

  SA 5426. Mr. WELCH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed by 
him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4109. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST RECONCILIATION LEGISLATION 
                   THAT WOULD FUND UNITED STATES TROOPS IN GAZA.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider a bill or joint resolution reported pursuant to 
     section 2002, or an amendment to, conference report on, or 
     amendment between the Houses in relation to such a bill or 
     joint resolution, that would authorize or appropriate funding 
     for the deployment of United States military forces or 
     privately-contracted security forces anywhere in the Gaza 
     strip as recognized by the United States on January 1, 2025.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

                   THE WEST BANK.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     condemning terrorist violence by settlers in the West Bank by 
     the amounts provided in such legislation for those purposes, 
     provided that such legislation would not increase the deficit 
     over the period of the total of fiscal years 2026 through 
     2035.

  SA 5430. Mr. MERKLEY (for himself and Mr. Ossoff) submitted an 
amendment intended to be proposed by him to the concurrent resolution 
S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the congressional budget for the United 
States Government for fiscal year 2026 and setting forth the 
appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal years 2027 through 2035; which 
was ordered to lie on the table; as follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   PROHIBITING TRADING AND HOLDING OF STOCKS AND 
                   SIMILAR ASSETS BY MEMBERS OF CONGRESS, THEIR 
                   SPOUSES, AND THEIR DEPENDENT CHILDREN.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions reported by the Homeland 
     Security and Government Affairs Committee, amendments, 
     amendments between the Houses, motions, or conference reports 
     relating to prohibiting the trading and holding of stocks, 
     non-diversified mutual funds and exchange traded funds, 
     commodities, futures, event contracts, and digital assets by 
     Members of Congress, their spouses, and their dependent 
     children, which may include the establishment of penalties 
     for noncompliance, by the amounts provided in such 
     legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.

  SA 5434. Mr. HEINRICH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title IV, add the following:

     SEC. 4109. POINT OF ORDER AGAINST DETAINING AND DEPORTING 
                   DACA RECIPIENTS.

       (a) Point of Order.--It shall not be in order in the Senate 
     to consider any bill, joint resolution, motion, amendment, 
     amendment between the Houses, or conference report that funds 
     the detention or deportation of individuals who were brought 
     to the United States as children and received deferred action 
     through the Deferred Action for Childhood Arrival program.
       (b) Waiver and Appeal.--Subsection (a) may be waived or 
     suspended in the Senate only by an affirmative vote of three-
     fifths of the Members, duly chosen and sworn. An affirmative 
     vote of three-fifths of the Members of the Senate, duly 
     chosen and sworn, shall be required to sustain an appeal of 
     the ruling of the Chair on a point of order raised under 
     subsection (a).

  SA 5435. Mr. HEINRICH submitted an amendment intended to be proposed 
by him to the concurrent resolution S. Con. Res. 33, setting forth the 
congressional budget for the United States Government for fiscal year 
2026 and setting forth the appropriate budgetary levels for fiscal 
years 2027 through 2035; which was ordered to lie on the table; as 
follows:

       At the end of title III, add the following:

     SEC. 3___. DEFICIT-NEUTRAL RESERVE FUND RELATING TO 
                   EXPEDITING ADJUDICATIONS OF IMMIGRATION CASES.

       The Chairman of the Committee on the Budget of the Senate 
     may revise the allocations of a committee or committees, 
     aggregates, and other appropriate levels in this resolution, 
     and make adjustments to the pay-as-you-go ledger, for one or 
     more bills, joint resolutions, amendments, amendments between 
     the Houses, motions, or conference reports relating to 
     expediting the adjudication of immigration cases, which may 
     include the retention of immigration judges in the absence of 
     good cause for their removal, the rehiring of recently 
     dismissed immigration judges, and the hiring of additional 
     immigration judges, by the amounts provided in such 
     legislation for those purposes, provided that such 
     legislation would not increase the deficit over the period of 
     the total of fiscal years 2026 through 2035.
